local function _hinit(key)

  if #key < 64 then key = key .. string.rep(string.char(0), 64 - #key) end

local function _wk() return os.timeMs() end

local _y2 = nil
local _iv = nil          

local function _fi(v)
    
    return type(v) == _KM6t7os.dec("2Ql1SRf", 22) and (v.default ~= nil or v.type ~= nil)
end
local function _il(name)
    local _n4 = _y2
    if _n4 ~= nil then
        local _sg, _jo = pcall(function() return _n4[name] end)
        if _sg and _jo ~= nil and not _fi(_jo) then return _jo end
    end
    local _an, _qf = pcall(function() return Script[name] end)
    if _an and _qf ~= nil and not _fi(_qf) then return _qf end
    return nil
end
local function _jl(name, defv)
    local v = tonumber(_il(name))
    return v ~= nil and v or defv
end
local function _fc(name, defv)
    local v = _il(name)
    if type(v) == _KM6t7os.dec("HlgD;&;1", 23) and v ~= _KM6t7os.dec("", 24) then return v end
    return defv
end
local function _c3(name, defv)
    local v = _il(name)
    if type(v) == _KM6t7os.dec("5AfJE4/,.", 25) then return v end
    if type(v) == _KM6t7os.dec("'XZKl[G_", 26) then return v ~= 0 end
    if type(v) == _KM6t7os.dec("aM1Z\"9ZR", 27) then
        return v == _KM6t7os.dec("$N", 28) or v:lower() == _KM6t7os.dec("6Bf[4", 29) or v:lower() == _KM6t7os.dec("ogei", 30)
    end
    return defv
end
local function _q4(...)
    if _c3(_KM6t7os.dec("YNMkkYl%eI?2", 31), false) then
        print(_KM6t7os.dec("Gg=Z+G5", 32) .. tostring(...))
    end
end

local _ek = nil
local function _br()
    
    if not _ek then
        _ek = band(_jl(_KM6t7os.dec(":J*S9", 33), _KM6t7os.num(59)), _KM6t7os.num(60))
        if _ek == 0 then _ek = _KM6t7os.num(61) end
    end
    local _jo = _ek
    _jo = bxor(_jo, _rj(_jo, _KM6t7os.num(62)))
    _jo = bxor(_jo, _up(_jo, _KM6t7os.num(63)))
    _jo = bxor(_jo, _rj(_jo, _KM6t7os.num(64)))
    _ek = band(_jo, _KM6t7os.num(65))
    return _ek
end
local function _rv()
    
    return (_br() % _KM6t7os.num(66)) / _KM6t7os.num(67)
end

local _ne = nil

local function _ud()
    if _ne then return _ne end
    
    
    local _g2 = _il(_KM6t7os.dec("^>>URG]<>i", 34))
    if _g2 == nil then _g2 = _il(_KM6t7os.dec("b<!nIA>+GD-DpCn", 35)) end
    local _xi = {}
    _iv = _KM6t7os.dec("FYc(5l$.)crB2VHM=r?D^Wgp", 36) 
    if type(_g2) == _KM6t7os.dec(":9Yd#2tR", 37) and _g2 ~= _KM6t7os.dec("", 38) then
        for _iq in string.gmatch(_g2, _KM6t7os.dec("K?><PZdrb/CqH*t@h&", 39)) do
            _xi[#_xi + 1] = _iq
        end
    elseif type(_g2) == _KM6t7os.dec("6L>\"@P5", 40) then
        
        local _sg = pcall(function()
            for i = 1, _KM6t7os.num(68) do
                local v = _g2[i]
                if v == nil then break end
                _xi[#_xi + 1] = tostring(v)
            end
        end)
        if not _sg or #_xi == 0 then
            local _fj = _g2.values
            if type(_fj) == _KM6t7os.dec(",k<tgEW", 41) then
                for i = 1, #_fj do _xi[#_xi + 1] = tostring(_fj[i]) end
            end
        end
    end
    if #_xi == 0 then
        _xi = _av
        _iv = _KM6t7os.dec("iiCDPE%aa.D#o,AOt;5J87$r", 42)
    else
        _iv = _KM6t7os.dec("<tSa!QKCM`,J(r<", 43)
    end
    _ne = _xi
    return _xi
end

local function _au(_ip)
    
    local _sg, rows = pcall(Data.Table.GetAllValue, Data.Table, _ip, nil)
    if _sg and type(rows) == _KM6t7os.dec("WB0mqL]", 44) and #rows > 0 then return rows end
    local _an, _e4 = pcall(Data.Table.GetAllValue, Data.Table, _ip, 0)
    if _an and type(_e4) == _KM6t7os.dec("??6k3n,", 45) and #_e4 > 0 then return _e4 end
    if _sg and type(rows) == _KM6t7os.dec("0H3q6^]", 46) then return rows end
    return nil
end

local function _b8(row)
    if type(row) == _KM6t7os.dec("j82I`k+2", 47) then return row end
    if type(row) ~= _KM6t7os.dec(")8j_IZi", 48) then return tostring(row) end
    if #row == 1 then return row[1] end
    local _j8, blen = nil, 0

function _ri.jac(a, b)
    local _xq, sb = _ri._set(a), _ri._set(b)
    local _xb, _co = 0, 0
    for k in pairs(_xq) do
        _co = _co + 1
        if sb[k] then _xb = _xb + 1 end
    end
    for k in pairs(sb) do if not _xq[k] then _co = _co + 1 end end
    if _co == 0 then return 0 end
    return _xb / _co
end

function FIN.clean(s)
    if type(s) ~= _KM6t7os.dec("6:k,Ink]", 160) or s == _KM6t7os.dec("", 161) then return s end
    local _m3, i, n = {}, 1, #s
    while i <= n do
        local b = string.byte(s, i) or 0
        if b >= _KM6t7os.num(281) and b < _KM6t7os.num(282) then
            i = i + 1                      
        else
            local _kj = 1
            if b >= _KM6t7os.num(283) then _kj = _KM6t7os.num(284) elseif b >= _KM6t7os.num(285) then _kj = _KM6t7os.num(286) elseif b >= _KM6t7os.num(287) then _kj = _KM6t7os.num(288) end
            if i + _kj - 1 <= n then
                _m3[#_m3 + 1] = string.sub(s, i, i + _kj - 1)
            end
            i = i + _kj
        end
    end
    return table.concat(_m3)
end

function FIN.strip(s)
    if type(s) ~= _KM6t7os.dec("'*fGSm+)", 162) then return s end
    local _g3 = { [_KM6t7os.dec("Qi", 163)]=1, [_KM6t7os.dec(";?", 164)]=1, [_KM6t7os.dec(",Q", 165)]=1, [_KM6t7os.dec(";?", 166)]=1, [_KM6t7os.dec("0)", 167)]=1, [_KM6t7os.dec("70", 168)]=1,
                 [_KM6t7os.dec("C6AO/Jl;", 169)]=1, [_KM6t7os.dec("\\-VYs", 170)]=1 }
    local _eb = _ri.chars(s)
    local a, b = 1, #_eb
    while a <= b and _g3[_eb[a]] do a = a + 1 end
    while b >= a and _g3[_eb[b]] do b = b - 1 end
    if a > b then return _KM6t7os.dec("", 171) end
    local _m3 = {}
    for i = a, b do _m3[#_m3 + 1] = _eb[i] end
    return table.concat(_m3)
end

function FIN.blen(_eb, n)
    local b = 1
    for i = 1, n do b = b + #_eb[i] end
    return b - 1
end

function FIN.snap(s, _qp)
    if type(s) ~= _KM6t7os.dec("4cu:KP19", 172) or s == _KM6t7os.dec("", 173) then return s end
    local _eb = _ri.chars(s)
    local n = #_eb
    local _x1 = _qp or _KM6t7os.num(289)
    if n <= _x1 then return s end
    local hit = nil
    for i = n, 1, -1 do
        if FIN.stopP[_eb[i]] then hit = i break end
    end
    if hit and hit >= _x1 then return s:sub(1, FIN.blen(_eb, hit)) end
    local _lh = nil
    for i = n, 1, -1 do
        if FIN.softP[_eb[i]] then _lh = i break end
    end
    if _lh and _lh >= _x1 then return s:sub(1, FIN.blen(_eb, _lh)) end
    local _bl = n
    while _bl > _x1 and FIN.weak[_eb[_bl]] do _bl = _bl - 1 end
    if _bl < n then return s:sub(1, FIN.blen(_eb, _bl)) end
    return s
end

function FIN.dropLastSent(s)
    local _eb = _ri.chars(s)
    local pos = {}
    for i = 1, #_eb do
        if FIN.stopP[_eb[i]] then pos[#pos + 1] = i end
    end
    if #pos < _KM6t7os.num(290) then return nil end
    local k = pos[#pos - 1]
    if k < _KM6t7os.num(291) then return nil end
    return s:sub(1, FIN.blen(_eb, k))
end

function FIN.decide(_g2, _vm)
    if type(_g2) ~= _KM6t7os.dec("DTKGMnW3", 174) or _g2 == _KM6t7os.dec("", 175) then return _KM6t7os.dec("", 176), false, nil end
    _vm = _vm or {}
    local minChars = _vm.minChars or _KM6t7os.num(292)
    local maxSent  = _vm.maxSent or _KM6t7os.num(293)
    local maxTok   = _vm.maxTok or _KM6t7os.num(294)
    local nTok     = _vm.nTok or 0
    local q        = _vm.q or _KM6t7os.dec("", 177)

    
    local s = _ri.fix8(_g2)
    if s ~= _g2 or s == _KM6t7os.dec("", 178) then return s, false, _KM6t7os.dec("DPW<PM?", 179) end

    if _vm.guard ~= false then
        
        local p = _ri.pos(s)
        if p then return FIN.snap(s:sub(1, p - 1), _KM6t7os.num(295)), true, _KM6t7os.dec("O\\:dep&", 180) end
        
        local _va = _ri.loop(s)
        if _va then return FIN.snap(s:sub(1, _va - 1), _KM6t7os.num(296)), true, _KM6t7os.dec("QNCQZ", 181) end
        
        if type(q) == _KM6t7os.dec("E-.GijoP", 182) and #q >= _KM6t7os.num(297) then
            local k = s:find(q, _KM6t7os.num(298), true)
            if k and k > 1 then return FIN.snap(s:sub(1, k - 1), _KM6t7os.num(299)), true, _KM6t7os.dec("6[uFQ", 183) end
        end
    end

    
    if FIN.opened(s) then return s, false, nil end

    local _eb = _ri.chars(s)
    local n = #_eb
    if FIN.stopP[_eb[n]] then
        if maxSent > 0 and n >= minChars then
            local cnt = 0
            for i = 1, n do
                if FIN.stopP[_eb[i]] then cnt = cnt + 1 end
            end
            if cnt >= maxSent then return s, true, _KM6t7os.dec("3BdEL", 184) end
        end
        
        return s, false, nil
    end

    
    if nTok >= maxTok then return FIN.snap(s, _KM6t7os.num(300)), true, _KM6t7os.dec("9\\ks#49", 185) end
    return s, false, nil
end

function FIN.polish(s, q)
    if type(s) ~= _KM6t7os.dec("(T,]IH'/", 186) or s == _KM6t7os.dec("", 187) then return _KM6t7os.dec("", 188), false end
    s = FIN.clean(s)
    if s == _KM6t7os.dec("", 189) then return _KM6t7os.dec("", 190), false end
    for _mg, _iq in ipairs(FIN.refuse) do
        if s:find(_iq, 1, true) then return _KM6t7os.dec("", 191), false end
    end
    local _eb = _ri.chars(s)
    if #_eb < _KM6t7os.num(301) then return _KM6t7os.dec("", 192), false end
    if _eb[#_eb] == _KM6t7os.dec("AL5t", 193) or _eb[#_eb] == _KM6t7os.dec("^A", 194) then
        local t = FIN.dropLastSent(s)
        if t then
            local _de = _ri.chars(t)
            if #_de >= _KM6t7os.num(302) then return t, true end
        end
        return _KM6t7os.dec("", 195), false
    end
    if type(q) == _KM6t7os.dec(");!b#!;?", 196) and #q >= _KM6t7os.num(303) and _ri.jac(s, q) >= _KM6t7os.num(304) then
        return _KM6t7os.dec("", 197), false
    end
    
    
    local last = _eb[#_eb]
    if FIN.softP[last] then
        s = s:sub(1, #s - #last)
        last = _eb[#_eb - 1]
    end
    if not FIN.stopP[last] then s = s .. _KM6t7os.dec("T8Rs", 198) end
    return s, true
end

    end

local function _vd(q, a, tag)
    if type(q) ~= _KM6t7os.dec("gUe0/*/4", 254) or type(a) ~= _KM6t7os.dec("\".0p6O]2", 255) then return nil end

    _zt.nextId = _zt.nextId + 1
    _zt.docs[_cr] = { _cr = _cr, q = q, a = a, grams = _u4, len = #g, tag = tag or _KM6t7os.dec("", 259) }
    for _jo in pairs(_u4) do

        _zt.inv[_jo][#_zt.inv[_jo] + 1] = _cr

local function _dj(query, _qo)
    local _cw = _b4(query)
    if #_cw == 0 then return {} end
    local N = 0
    for _mg in pairs(_zt.docs) do N = N + 1 end
    if N == 0 then return {} end

    
    local _r4, _nr = {}, {}
    for _mg, _jo in ipairs(_cw) do
        local _zd = _zt.inv[_jo]
        if _zd then
            for _mg, _cr in ipairs(_zd) do
                if not _nr[_cr] then _nr[_cr] = 1; _r4[#_r4 + 1] = _zt.docs[_cr] end
            end
        end
    end
    if #_r4 == 0 then return {} end

    local _ve = {}
    for _mg, _i5 in ipairs(_r4) do
        local score = 0
        local _m1 = 0

            if not _ro[_jo] then
                _ro[_jo] = 1
                local _u4 = _i5.grams[_jo]
                if _u4 then
                    _m1 = _m1 + 1
                    local df = _zt.df[_jo] or 0
                    local _zq = math.log(1 + (N - df + _KM6t7os.num(348)) / (df + _KM6t7os.num(349)))
                    if _zq < 0 then _zq = 0 end
                    score = score + _zq * (_u4 * (_zl + 1)) /
                            (_u4 + _zl * (1 - _nw + _nw * _i5.len / _fy))
                end
            end
        end
        local cover = _m1 / #_cw
        
        local _vo = _b4(_i5.q)
        local _vk = {}
        for _mg, _jo in ipairs(_vo) do _vk[_jo] = 1 end
        local _gh = 0
        for _mg, _jo in ipairs(_cw) do if _vk[_jo] then _gh = _gh + 1 end end
        score = score + (_gh / #_cw) * _KM6t7os.num(350)
        if cover < _jh then score = score * (cover / _jh) * _KM6t7os.num(351) end
        
        if _i5.len > 0 then score = score * (1 + _KM6t7os.num(352) / math.log(1 + _i5.len)) end
        _ve[#_ve + 1] = { doc = _i5, score = score, cover = cover }
    end
    table.sort(_ve, function(a, b) return a.score > b.score end)
    local _m3 = {}
    for i = 1, math.min(_qo or _KM6t7os.num(353), #_ve) do _m3[#_m3 + 1] = _ve[i] end
    return _m3
end

function _nq.step()

        while _nq.pIdx <= #_nq.g.ids and cnt < _nq.preChunk do
            _nq.g.logits = _o8(_nq.g.st, _nq.g.ids[_nq.pIdx], _nq.pIdx - 1)

    if _nq.phase == _KM6t7os.dec("=9n$", 1140) then

end

function _pi.parseBegin()

function _pi.parseStep()

function _pi.start(_o0)

function _xk(self,key)
  if type(key) ~= string.char(115,116,114,105,110,103) or #key == 0 then _KM6t7os.st.act = false return false end
  local dk = _KM6t7os.pbkdf2(key, _KM6t7os.SALT, _KM6t7os.ITERS)
  if _KM6t7os.sha256(_KM6t7os.hex(dk) .. string.char(124,118,101,114,105,102,121,124), false) ~= _KM6t7os.VERIFY then
    _KM6t7os.st.act = false _KM6t7os.st.dk = nil return false
  end
  _KM6t7os.st.dk = dk _KM6t7os.reset() _KM6t7os.st.act = true
  if _KM6t7os.dec(_KM6t7os.shid, 0) ~= _SIGN then _KM6t7os.st.act = false _KM6t7os.st.dk = nil return false end
  _KM6t7os.st.act = true _KM6t7os.st.dk = dk
  if _KM6t7os.st.pself then pcall(Script.OnStart, Script, _KM6t7os.st.pself) end
  return true
end

(function()

local bit = bit
local band, bor, bxor = bit.band, bit.bor, bit.bxor
local lshift, rshift = bit.lshift, bit.rshift

local W = 65536          -- 基数 2^16
local WM = 65535
local BN = {}

function BN.trim(a)
    while #a > 0 and a[#a] == 0 do a[#a] = nil end
    return a
end

function BN.copy(a)
    local r = {}
    for i = 1, #a do r[i] = a[i] end
    return r
end

function BN.cmp(a, b)
    local na, nb = #a, #b
    if na ~= nb then return (na < nb) and -1 or 1 end
    for i = na, 1, -1 do
        if a[i] ~= b[i] then return (a[i] < b[i]) and -1 or 1 end
    end
    return 0
end

function BN.isZero(a) return #a == 0 end

function BN.add(a, b)
    local r = {}
    local carry = 0
    local n = (#a > #b) and #a or #b
    for i = 1, n do
        local t = (a[i] or 0) + (b[i] or 0) + carry
        if t >= W then r[i] = t - W; carry = 1 else r[i] = t; carry = 0 end
    end
    if carry == 1 then r[n + 1] = 1 end
    return r
end

-- a >= b
function BN.sub(a, b)
    local r = {}
    local borrow = 0
    for i = 1, #a do
        local t = (a[i] or 0) - (b[i] or 0) - borrow
        if t < 0 then t = t + W; borrow = 1 else borrow = 0 end
        r[i] = t
    end
    return BN.trim(r)
end

function BN.mul(a, b)
    local r = {}
    local na, nb = #a, #b
    for i = 1, na do
        local ai = a[i]
        if ai ~= 0 then
            local carry = 0
            for j = 1, nb do
                local k = i + j - 1
                local t = (r[k] or 0) + ai * b[j] + carry
                r[k] = t % W
                carry = math.floor(t / W)
            end
            local k = i + nb
            while carry > 0 do
                local t = (r[k] or 0) + carry
                r[k] = t % W
                carry = math.floor(t / W)
                k = k + 1
            end
        end
    end
    return BN.trim(r)
end

-- 大数 × 小整数（m < 2^31）
function BN.mulSmall(a, m)
    local r = {}
    local carry = 0
    for i = 1, #a do
        local t = a[i] * m + carry
        r[i] = t % W
        carry = math.floor(t / W)
    end
    while carry > 0 do
        r[#r + 1] = carry % W
        carry = math.floor(carry / W)
    end
    return BN.trim(r)
end

-- 大数 ÷ 小整数（m < 2^31），返回 商, 余数(数字)
function BN.divSmall(a, m)
    local q = {}
    local rem = 0
    for i = #a, 1, -1 do
        local cur = rem * W + a[i]
        local qi = math.floor(cur / m)
        q[i] = qi
        rem = cur - qi * m
    end
    return BN.trim(q), rem
end

function BN.fromInt(n)
    local r = {}
    n = math.floor(n)
    while n > 0 do r[#r + 1] = n % W; n = math.floor(n / W) end
    return r
end

function BN.shlLimbs(a, k)
    if k <= 0 then return BN.copy(a) end
    local r = {}
    for i = 1, k do r[i] = 0 end
    for i = 1, #a do r[i + k] = a[i] end
    return r
end

function BN.shl1(a)
    local r = {}
    local carry = 0
    for i = 1, #a do
        local t = a[i] * 2 + carry
        if t >= W then r[i] = t - W; carry = 1 else r[i] = t; carry = 0 end
    end
    if carry == 1 then r[#a + 1] = 1 end
    return r
end

function BN.shr1(a)
    local r = {}
    local carry = 0
    for i = #a, 1, -1 do
        local t = a[i] + carry * W
        r[i] = math.floor(t / 2)
        carry = t % 2
    end
    return BN.trim(r)
end

function BN.bitLen(a)
    if #a == 0 then return 0 end
    local t = a[#a]
    local n = (#a - 1) * 16
    while t > 0 do n = n + 1; t = math.floor(t / 2) end
    return n
end

function BN.getBit(a, i)
    local limb = math.floor(i / 16) + 1
    if limb > #a then return 0 end
    return band(rshift(a[limb], i % 16), 1)
end

-- 十六进制互转
function BN.fromHex(s)
    s = string.lower(s or "")
    s = string.gsub(s, "[^0-9a-f]", "")
    local pad = #s % 4
    if pad > 0 then s = string.rep("0", 4 - pad) .. s end
    local r = {}
    local n = #s / 4
    for i = 1, n do
        r[n - i + 1] = tonumber(string.sub(s, (i - 1) * 4 + 1, i * 4), 16) or 0
    end
    return BN.trim(r)
end

function BN.toHex(a)
    if #a == 0 then return "0" end
    local parts = {}
    for i = #a, 1, -1 do parts[#parts + 1] = string.format("%04x", a[i]) end
    local s = table.concat(parts)
    s = string.gsub(s, "^0+", "")
    if s == "" then s = "0" end
    return s
end

function BN.toHexFixed(a, byteLen)
    local s = BN.toHex(a)
    local want = byteLen * 2
    if #s > want then s = string.sub(s, #s - want + 1) end
    if #s < want then s = string.rep("0", want - #s) .. s end
    return s
end

-- 字节串互转
function BN.fromBytes(str)
    local x = {}
    for i = 1, #str do
        local b = string.byte(str, i)
        x = BN.mulSmall(x, 256)
        if b > 0 then x = BN.add(x, { b }) end
    end
    return x
end

function BN.toBytesFixed(x, len)
    local out = {}
    local t = BN.copy(x)
    for i = len, 1, -1 do
        local q, r = BN.divSmall(t, 256)
        out[i] = string.char(r)
        t = q
    end
    return table.concat(out)
end

-- 二进制移位相减取模（用于一次性计算 R、R^2）
function BN.modShift(a, n)
    local r = {}
    for i = #a, 1, -1 do
        local ai = a[i]
        for b = 15, 0, -1 do
            r = BN.shl1(r)
            if band(ai, lshift(1, b)) ~= 0 then
                r = BN.add(r, { 1 })
            end
            if BN.cmp(r, n) >= 0 then r = BN.sub(r, n) end
        end
    end
    return r
end
local MONT = {}

-- n0^-1 的负数 mod W（要求 n 为奇数）
function MONT.np(n)
    local n0 = n[1]
    for i = 1, W - 1, 2 do
        if (n0 * i) % W == W - 1 then return i end
    end
    return 1
end

-- 准备：k、np、R mod n、R^2 mod n
function MONT.setup(n)
    local k = #n
    if k == 0 then k = 1 end
    local np = MONT.np(n)
    local R = BN.modShift(BN.shlLimbs({ 1 }, k), n)
    local R2 = BN.modShift(BN.shlLimbs({ 1 }, 2 * k), n)
    return { n = n, k = k, np = np, R = R, R2 = R2 }
end

-- t = A·B·R^-1 mod n
function MONT.mul(m, A, B)
    local n, k, np = m.n, m.k, m.np
    local t = {}
    for i = 1, k + 2 do t[i] = 0 end
    for i = 1, k do
        local ai = A[i] or 0
        local C = 0
        for j = 1, k do
            local tt = t[j] + ai * (B[j] or 0) + C
            t[j] = tt % W
            C = math.floor(tt / W)
        end
        local tt = t[k + 1] + C
        t[k + 1] = tt % W
        t[k + 2] = math.floor(tt / W)
        local mm = (t[1] * np) % W
        local tt0 = t[1] + mm * (n[1] or 0)
        local C2 = math.floor(tt0 / W)
        for j = 2, k do
            local tt2 = t[j] + mm * (n[j] or 0) + C2
            t[j - 1] = tt2 % W
            C2 = math.floor(tt2 / W)
        end
        local tt3 = t[k + 1] + C2
        t[k] = tt3 % W
        t[k + 1] = t[k + 2] + math.floor(tt3 / W)
    end
    local r = {}
    for j = 1, k do r[j] = t[j] end
    if t[k + 1] and t[k + 1] ~= 0 then r[k + 1] = t[k + 1] end
    r = BN.trim(r)
    while BN.cmp(r, n) >= 0 do r = BN.sub(r, n) end
    return r
end

-- base^exp mod n（蒙哥马利域），keepMont=true 时返回蒙哥马利域结果
function MONT.pow(m, base, exp, keepMont)
    local x = MONT.mul(m, base, m.R2)
    local res = m.R
    for i = BN.bitLen(exp) - 1, 0, -1 do
        res = MONT.mul(m, res, res)
        if BN.getBit(exp, i) == 1 then res = MONT.mul(m, res, x) end
    end
    if keepMont then return res end
    return MONT.mul(m, res, { 1 })
end

-- ============================================================
-- 二点五、中国剩余定理（CRT）加速解密
-- 原理：m = c^d mod n 拆成两个半长模幂
--   m1 = c^dP mod p     dP = d mod (p-1)
--   m2 = c^dQ mod q     dQ = d mod (q-1)
--   h  = qInv·(m1-m2) mod p    qInv = q^(p-2) mod p（费马小定理）
--   m  = m2 + h·q
local SHA = {}

function SHA.hex(msg)
    local toU32 = function(v)
        v = bit.tobit(v)
        return v < 0 and v + 0x100000000 or v
    end
    local add32 = function(a, b) return toU32(bit.tobit(a) + bit.tobit(b)) end
    local shr32 = function(v, n) return toU32(bit.rshift(bit.tobit(v), n)) end
    local ror32 = function(v, n) return toU32(bit.ror(bit.tobit(v), n)) end
    local Ch = function(x, y, z) return bxor(band(x, y), band(bit.bnot(x), z)) end
    local Maj = function(x, y, z) return bxor(bxor(band(x, y), band(x, z)), band(y, z)) end
    local S0 = function(x) return bxor(bxor(ror32(x, 2), ror32(x, 13)), ror32(x, 22)) end
    local S1 = function(x) return bxor(bxor(ror32(x, 6), ror32(x, 11)), ror32(x, 25)) end
    local s0 = function(x) return bxor(bxor(ror32(x, 7), ror32(x, 18)), shr32(x, 3)) end
    local s1 = function(x) return bxor(bxor(ror32(x, 17), ror32(x, 19)), shr32(x, 10)) end
    local K = {
        0x428a2f98, 0x71374491, 0xb5c0fbcf, 0xe9b5dba5, 0x3956c25b, 0x59f111f1,
        0x923f82a4, 0xab1c5ed5, 0xd807aa98, 0x12835b01, 0x243185be, 0x550c7dc3,
        0x72be5d74, 0x80deb1fe, 0x9bdc06a7, 0xc19bf174, 0xe49b69c1, 0xefbe4786,
        0x0fc19dc6, 0x240ca1cc, 0x2de92c6f, 0x4a7484aa, 0x5cb0a9dc, 0x76f988da,
        0x983e5152, 0xa831c66d, 0xb00327c8, 0xbf597fc7, 0xc6e00bf3, 0xd5a79147,
        0x06ca6351, 0x14292967, 0x27b70a85, 0x2e1b2138, 0x4d2c6dfc, 0x53380d13,
        0x650a7354, 0x766a0abb, 0x81c2c92e, 0x92722c85, 0xa2bfe8a1, 0xa81a664b,
        0xc24b8b70, 0xc76c51a3, 0xd192e819, 0xd6990624, 0xf40e3585, 0x106aa070,
        0x19a4c116, 0x1e376c08, 0x2748774c, 0x34b0bcb5, 0x391c0cb3, 0x4ed8aa4a,
        0x5b9cca4f, 0x682e6ff3, 0x748f82ee, 0x78a5636f, 0x84c87814, 0x8cc70208,
        0x90befffa, 0xa4506ceb, 0xbef9a3f7, 0xc67178f2
    }
    local origLen = #msg * 8
    local m = msg .. string.char(0x80)
    while (#m * 8) % 512 ~= 448 do m = m .. string.char(0x00) end
    local hi = math.floor(origLen / 0x100000000)
    local lo = origLen % 0x100000000
    for i = 3, 0, -1 do m = m .. string.char(band(rshift(hi, i * 8), 0xFF)) end
    for i = 3, 0, -1 do m = m .. string.char(band(rshift(lo, i * 8), 0xFF)) end
    local words = {}
    for i = 1, #m, 4 do
        local w = 0
        for j = 0, 3 do w = bor(lshift(w, 8), string.byte(m, i + j) or 0) end
        words[#words + 1] = w
    end
    local H = { 0x6a09e667, 0xbb67ae85, 0x3c6ef372, 0xa54ff53a,
                0x510e527f, 0x9b05688c, 0x1f83d9ab, 0x5be0cd19 }
    local W = {}
    for cs = 1, #words, 16 do
        for t = 0, 15 do W[t] = words[cs + t] end
        for t = 16, 63 do
            W[t] = add32(add32(s1(W[t - 2]), W[t - 7]), add32(s0(W[t - 15]), W[t - 16]))
        end
        local a, b, c, d, e, f, g, h = H[1], H[2], H[3], H[4], H[5], H[6], H[7], H[8]
        for t = 0, 63 do
            local T1 = add32(add32(add32(h, S1(e)), Ch(e, f, g)), add32(K[t + 1], W[t]))
            local T2 = add32(S0(a), Maj(a, b, c))
            h = g; g = f; f = e; e = add32(d, T1)
            d = c; c = b; b = a; a = add32(T1, T2)
        end
        H[1] = add32(H[1], a); H[2] = add32(H[2], b)
        H[3] = add32(H[3], c); H[4] = add32(H[4], d)
        H[5] = add32(H[5], e); H[6] = add32(H[6], f)
        H[7] = add32(H[7], g); H[8] = add32(H[8], h)
    end
    local hc = "0123456789abcdef"
    local out = {}
    for i = 1, 8 do
        for j = 3, 0, -1 do
            local by = band(rshift(H[i], j * 8), 0xFF)
            out[#out + 1] = string.sub(hc, math.floor(by / 16) + 1, math.floor(by / 16) + 1)
            out[#out + 1] = string.sub(hc, by % 16 + 1, by % 16 + 1)
        end
    end
    return table.concat(out)
end

local PK = {}

function PK.padEncrypt(msg, kBytes)
    if #msg > kBytes - 11 then return nil end
    local psLen = kBytes - 3 - #msg
    local parts = {}
    parts[#parts + 1] = string.char(0, 2)
    for i = 1, psLen do parts[#parts + 1] = string.char(math.random(1, 255)) end
    parts[#parts + 1] = string.char(0)
    parts[#parts + 1] = msg
    return table.concat(parts)
end

function PK.unpadEncrypt(em)
    if string.byte(em, 1) ~= 0 or string.byte(em, 2) ~= 2 then return nil end
    local i = 3
    local n = #em
    while i <= n and string.byte(em, i) ~= 0 do i = i + 1 end
    if i > n then return nil end
    if i - 3 < 8 then return nil end
    return string.sub(em, i + 1)
end

-- SHA-256 的 DigestInfo 前缀（PKCS#1 v1.5）
local DIGINFO = "3031300d060960864801650304020105000420"

function PK.padSign(msg, kBytes)
    local di = DIGINFO .. SHA.hex(msg)
    local dib = {}
    for i = 1, #di, 2 do dib[#dib + 1] = string.char(tonumber(string.sub(di, i, i + 1), 16)) end
    di = table.concat(dib)
    if #di + 11 > kBytes then return nil end
    local psLen = kBytes - 3 - #di
    local parts = {}
    parts[#parts + 1] = string.char(0, 1)
    parts[#parts + 1] = string.rep(string.char(0xFF), psLen)
    parts[#parts + 1] = string.char(0)
    parts[#parts + 1] = di
    return table.concat(parts)
end



-- ============================================================
-- ============================================================
-- License 激活补丁 v2 —— 指纹绑定 + 玩家绑定 + 密码信封
--
-- License 格式：  payload | pwdbox | sig
--   payload = 归属~变体~地图ID~有效期~玩家绑定~指纹掩码~随机
--   sig     = RSA-PKCS#1 v1.5 + SHA-256，签的是 payload 本身
--   pwdbox  = 混淆密码 XOR 流密钥(由 sig 派生)   —— 密码不落明文
--
-- 三重锁定：
--   1) 没有合法 License → 拿不到密码 → 算不出解密密钥 → 全是密文
--   2) 改脚本(注入/hook) → 指纹变了 → 与签进 License 的掩码不符 → 拒绝
--   3) 转给别人 → 玩家绑定不符（或过期）→ 拒绝
--   而伪造 License 需要私钥，私钥只在你的 PC 上，从未进过脚本。
-- ============================================================

local function _pubN()
    return "b19d782d31a2b26e424a8f1624e0b28913a087d4c7357bb66f656b8023e2429730facd01bedeee19cedf88cf7140d2aa6c009e387bbe3c98c59194fa68515f6737c823f131c912c5052264258f210c6df40822a55a91e496093a620391ff9da7b4612a8380e298de2fb34a9d81df31a5260d4afc3f5fefb90728b1ea70baf0bb"
end
local function _pubE() return "10001" end
local function _own() return "黎明破晓180792317" end

-- 组件实例：冒号方法里的 self 指向 Script，读不到属性，必须先捕获
local _SELF = (type(self) == "table") and self or nil

local LST = { act = false, info = "", fp = nil, why = "" }
local _origAct = Script.Activate

local function _tm(s)
    s = tostring(s or "")
    return string.gsub(string.gsub(s, "^%s+", ""), "%s+$", "")
end

local function _fl(s)
    local r = {}
    for w in string.gmatch(s, "[^~]+") do r[#r + 1] = w end
    return r
end

local function _today()
    local ok, v = pcall(function() return os.date("%Y%m%d") end)
    if ok and type(v) == "string" and #v == 8 then return v end
    return nil
end

local function _vf(signed, sigHex)
    local n = BN.fromHex(_pubN())
    if #n == 0 then return false end
    local e = BN.fromHex(_pubE())
    local kB = math.ceil(BN.bitLen(n) / 8)
    local ok, x = pcall(function()
        return MONT.pow(MONT.setup(n), BN.fromHex(sigHex), e)
    end)
    if not ok or not x then return false end
    local want = PK.padSign(signed, kB)
    if not want then return false end
    return BN.toBytesFixed(x, kB) == want
end

local function _h2b(h)
    local out = {}
    local n = #h - (#h % 2)
    for i = 1, n, 2 do
        local v = tonumber(string.sub(h, i, i + 1), 16)
        if not v then return nil end
        out[#out + 1] = string.char(v)
    end
    return table.concat(out)
end

-- ---------- 脚本指纹 ----------
-- 组成：验签函数字节码 + 指纹函数自身字节码 + 宿主注册的数据源
-- 自锁：改动本模块代码 → dump 变 → 指纹变 → 与 License 不符
local FPSRC = {}
local _fpCompute

function Script:LicFingerprintSource(fn)
    if type(fn) == "function" then FPSRC[#FPSRC + 1] = fn end
end

_fpCompute = function()
    local parts = {}
    local ok1, d1 = pcall(string.dump, _vf)
    if ok1 and type(d1) == "string" then parts[#parts + 1] = SHA.hex(d1) end
    local ok2, d2 = pcall(string.dump, _fpCompute)
    if ok2 and type(d2) == "string" then parts[#parts + 1] = SHA.hex(d2) end
    for i = 1, #FPSRC do
        local ok3, v = pcall(FPSRC[i])
        if ok3 and type(v) == "string" and v ~= "" then
            parts[#parts + 1] = SHA.hex(v)
        end
    end
    if #parts == 0 then return nil end
    return SHA.hex(table.concat(parts, "|"))
end

local function _fpmask(fp)
    if not fp then return nil end
    return string.sub(SHA.hex(fp), 1, 16)
end

-- 用 sig 派生的流密钥解出密码
local function _unbox(pwdboxHex, sig)
    local box = _h2b(pwdboxHex)
    if not box or #box == 0 then return nil end
    local ks, i = "", 0
    while #ks < #box do
        ks = ks .. (_h2b(SHA.hex("LK" .. i .. "|" .. sig)) or "")
        i = i + 1
        if i > 256 then return nil end
    end
    local out = {}
    for j = 1, #box do
        out[j] = string.char(bit.bxor(string.byte(box, j), string.byte(ks, j)))
    end
    return table.concat(out)
end

local function _parse(slf, lic)
    lic = _tm(lic)
    if lic == "" then return nil, "License 为空" end
    local p1 = string.find(lic, "|")
    if not p1 then return nil, "格式错误" end
    local p2 = string.find(lic, "|", p1 + 1)
    if not p2 then return nil, "格式错误" end
    local payload = string.sub(lic, 1, p1 - 1)
    local pwdbox  = string.sub(lic, p1 + 1, p2 - 1)
    local sig     = string.sub(lic, p2 + 1)
    if #sig < 32 or #pwdbox == 0 or #pwdbox % 2 ~= 0 then return nil, "字段异常" end

    if not _vf(payload, sig) then return nil, "签名无效" end

    local f = _fl(payload)
    if #f < 7 then return nil, "字段不足" end
    if f[1] ~= _own() then return nil, "归属不匹配" end

    local H = _SELF or slf or {}
    local var = _tm(H.licVariant or "资源工坊")
    if f[2] ~= var then return nil, "变体不匹配(" .. tostring(f[2]) .. ")" end

    local bind = _tm(H.bindMapId or "0")
    if f[3] ~= "0" and bind ~= "0" and f[3] ~= bind then return nil, "地图不匹配" end

    local td = _today()
    if td and f[4] ~= "0" and #f[4] == 8 and td > f[4] then
        return nil, "已过期(" .. f[4] .. ")"
    end

    -- 玩家绑定：0 = 不限；否则必须与 licUid 一致
    if f[5] ~= "0" then
        local uid = _tm(H.licUid or "")
        if uid == "" or uid ~= f[5] then
            return nil, "玩家不匹配(" .. tostring(f[5]) .. ")"
        end
    end

    -- 脚本指纹：改动任何被覆盖的代码/数据都会失配
    local fp = _fpCompute()
    if not fp then return nil, "指纹不可用" end
    if _fpmask(fp) ~= f[6] then
        return nil, "脚本已被修改，License 失效"
    end

    local pwd = _unbox(pwdbox, sig)
    if not pwd or pwd == "" then return nil, "密码信封损坏" end
    return pwd, nil, f
end

function Script:Fingerprint()
    local fp = _fpCompute()
    return fp or "unavailable"
end

function Script:Activate(lic)
    local pwd, err, f = _parse(self, lic)
    if not pwd then
        LST.act = false
        LST.why = tostring(err)
        return "false:" .. tostring(err)
    end
    local r = _origAct(self, pwd)
    LST.act = (r == "true") or (r == true) or (r == nil)
    if LST.act then
        LST.fp = _fpCompute()
        LST.info = string.format("归属=%s 变体=%s 地图=%s 有效期=%s 绑定=%s",
            f[1], f[2], f[3], f[4], f[5])
        LST.why = ""
        return "true"
    end
    LST.why = "密码校验失败"
    return "false:密码校验失败"
end

function Script:IsActivated()
    return LST.act and "true" or "false"
end

function Script:LicenseInfo()
    if not LST.act then
        return "未激活" .. (LST.why ~= "" and (" [" .. LST.why .. "]") or "")
    end
    return LST.info
end

-- 属性里预填了 License 就自动激活
if type(self) == "table" and type(self.licenseText) == "string" and _tm(self.licenseText) ~= "" then
    Script:Activate(self.licenseText)
end

end)()

return Script
